- **alignment-level-QC**: Calculates technical mapping statistics for any aligned BAM file (ChIP or ATAC). Do NOT use this skill for biological signal validation (like checking for peaks or open chromatin) or for filtering/removing reads. - **allele-abundance-estimation**: Use when you must quantify how accurately an MLST deconvolution pipeline recovers fractional allele composition and complete strain types from simulated mixed reads, and need a baseline comparison against abundance-estimation tools like Kallisto or strainEST. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-abundance-estimation))
- **allele-based-read-scoring-and-haplotype-reweighting**: Use when raw long reads (Nanopore R9/R10 or PacBio CLR) from a diploid genome must be error-corrected where the sequencing error rate exceeds haplotype divergence and heterozygote alleles would otherwise be collapsed into the consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-based-read-scoring-and-haplotype-reweighting))
- **allele-call-accuracy-evaluation-tvd**: Use when validating an allele-diversity or strain-deconvolution stage against known simulated allele mixtures, and you need per-locus precision/recall and TVD statistics to compare with published baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-call-accuracy-evaluation-tvd))
- **allele-depth-pileup-processing**: Use when shotgun metagenomic BAMs aligned to a reference gene database must be converted into allele-depth pile-ups (cellSNP VCF), clustered via MetaQuad, and normalized into per-gene nucleotide diversity for group comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-depth-pileup-processing))
- **allele-diversity-integer-linear-programming**: Use when WGS reads have been mapped to a curated MLST allele database and you must infer, per locus, which alleles are present and their fractional proportions in mixed (within-host heterogeneous) samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-diversity-integer-linear-programming))
- **allele-fasta-sequence-processing**: Use when you have one FASTA file per locus with all identified alleles from a chewBBACA cg/wgMLST schema and need to assess allelic variability per locus via per-locus MSAs and phylogenetic trees. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-fasta-sequence-processing))
- **allele-frequency-binned-constraint-regression**: Use when gnomAD SNVs overlap fine-mapped eCLIP enriched windows and you need to test whether disruptive variants are depleted from factor-bound sequences as evidence of constraint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-frequency-binned-constraint-regression))
- **allele-frequency-density-clustering**: Use when you have cellsnp-lite AD/DP allele count matrices from shotgun metagenomic samples and need to identify shared informative variants versus background noise based on clustering of sample allele frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-density-clustering))
- **allele-frequency-dissimilarity-computation**: Use when comparing treatment groups (e.g. CLA, LEVO vs OTHERS) at a time point such as T+6W using allele-frequency profiles of shared informative variants from a cellSNP-lite/MetaQuad pipeline on ARG VCF data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-dissimilarity-computation))
- **allele-frequency-extraction-from-vcf**: Use when VCF files from low-frequency variant calling on wastewater NGS samples must be matched against a lineage mutation profile (e.g., B.1.1.7 unique mutations via Pangolin rules or outbreak.info) and per-mutation allele frequencies or a non-zero minimum AF are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-extraction-from-vcf))
- **allele-frequency-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have vireoSNP clone labels and a set of MQuad ΔBIC-selected mtDNA variants and need to visually confirm clonal discrimination, e.g. reproducing Fig. 4c for MKN-45 scDNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-frequency-heatmap-visualization))
- **allele-frequency-matching-of-variant-sets**: Use when building labeled variant training sets for a variant-effect or disease-association classifier and you need negative controls frequency-matched to positives (MAF thresholds or matched MAF distributions) to avoid allele-frequency artifacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-frequency-matching-of-variant-sets))
- **allele-frequency-order-of-magnitude-comparison**: Use when comparing population allele frequencies (e.g., gnomAD AF) between the previous and current annotation snapshots of a submitted variant, to decide whether the shift is large enough to notify the user or should be silently ignored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-order-of-magnitude-comparison))
- **allele-frequency-summary-statistics**: Use when you have VCF files of SARS-CoV-2 variants (e.g., from freebayes on wastewater NGS data) and need per-lineage abundance metrics such as mean allele frequency and variant counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-summary-statistics))
- **allele-frequency-threshold-classification**: Use when a resistance-calling pipeline must distinguish susceptible, low-frequency (minor), and high-frequency (major) resistant alleles from k-mer depths at mutation sites, and when a frequency threshold for calling resistance must be applied or justified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-threshold-classification))
- **allele-frequency-trajectory-simulation**: Use when the task requires simulating or visualizing how allele frequencies change through time at a biallelic locus under selection, drift, or their combination — e.g., reproducing dominance-regime dynamics (overdominance, underdominance, codominance) or Wright–Fisher drift-selection trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-frequency-trajectory-simulation))
- **allele-proportion-error-constraint-modeling**: Use when reconstructing a multi-sample strain decomposition from per-locus allele distributions (geneX_proportions.csv) where you must minimize previously unobserved strains while respecting allele proportions within a tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-proportion-error-constraint-modeling))
- **allele-proportion-estimation-from-read-mappings**: Use when processing WGS tick samples (2x76bp read pairs) against the 1726-allele B. burgdorferi MLST database and you need, per locus, the set of alleles and their fractional composition under within-host strain heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allele-proportion-estimation-from-read-mappings))
- **allele-sequence-similarity-pairing**: Use when you have called SV VCFs (e.g. from VolcanoSV, cuteSV, Sniffles2, pbsv, DeBreak) and a GIAB-style truth set, and need to measure how F1 depends on Truvari matching thresholds plus how accurately TP pairs recover breakpoints and allele sequences. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-sequence-similarity-pairing))
- **allele-specific-cna-detection**: Use when analyzing PTA-amplified high-coverage (&gt;20x) scWGS BAMs with matched bulk WGS, to call small (200 kb–5 Mb) allele-specific CNAs that RDR-only callers miss and to obtain integer copy-number states per allele per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-cna-detection))
- **allele-specific-cna-detection-from-scrnaseq**: Use when the goal is to infer haplotype-aware CNA states or tumor subclones from single-cell RNA-seq count data (e.g., 10x Genomics droplet or Smart-seq2 tumor samples), especially when copy-neutral LOH or low tumor purity challenges expression-only methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-cna-detection-from-scrnaseq))
- **allele-specific-copy-number-inference**: Use when analyzing high-coverage (&gt;20x) PTA-amplified single-cell WGS data where small (100 kb–Mb scale) somatic CNAs must be called with allele-specific copy states, and paired bulk WGS is available for phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-copy-number-inference))
- **allele-specific-copy-number-state-inference**: Use when per-cell copy gain/loss/LOH states must be inferred from raw scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq counts (10x or Smart-seq2), especially for tumour subclone dissection or when benchmarking XClone against InferCNV/CopyKAT/CaSpER/Numbat with AUROC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-copy-number-state-inference))
- **allele-specific-expression-detection**: Use when multi-sample VCF files (GATK HaplotypeCaller output) from RNA-seq of heterozygous individuals are available and the goal is to identify which biallelic SNPs show statistically significant allele-specific expression per tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-expression-detection))
- **allele-specific-expression-gene-set-compilation**: Use when you have VADT sig_meta_analysis_variants.txt call sets for multiple tissues and need gene-level ASE sets, cross-tissue overlap percentages, or a high-ASE-score gene list for pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-expression-gene-set-compilation))
- **allele-specific-haplotype-assignment**: Use when ChIA-PET (or similar chromatin interaction) data must be resolved into haplotype-specific peaks and loops for a genome whose SNP phasing information is available, and allelic bias in binding coverage needs statistical testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-haplotype-assignment))
- **allele-specific-methylation-testing**: Use when haplotagged nanopore reads carry per-read mCG log-likelihood-ratio or 6mA-region calls and you need to find regions (CGIs, ChIP peaks) with statistically significant allele-specific methylation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-specific-methylation-testing))
- **allele-specific-methylation-visualization**: Use when WGBS reads have been aligned and methylation-called with Msuite and you need per-read, genotype-preserved methylation views at imprinted or allele-specific loci, e.g. to phase reads at a heterozygous A/G SNP and observe reciprocal methylation bars. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-methylation-visualization))
- **allele-specific-peptide-prioritization**: Use when patient-specific HLA allele lists (mhc-I.tsv / mhc-II.tsv) and per-variant-type variant_effects.tsv files are available and predicted neoantigens must be ranked per allele by MHC binding affinity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-peptide-prioritization))
- **allele-specific-pet-assignment**: Use when analyzing ChIA-PET bedpe data from a heterozygous sample (e.g., GM12878 with Illumina Platinum Genomes phased genotypes) to determine which parental allele mediates each chromatin loop. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-pet-assignment))
- **allele-specific-read-counting**: Use when scRNA-Seq reads from a polymorphic F1 cross (e.g. CAST×B6) must be converted into per-allele gene counts, and you must decide between unique-read counting and multi-read weighted allocation before allelic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-read-counting))
- **allele-specific-read-partitioning**: Use when ONT reads (e.g., HG002 R10.4 BAM) must be partitioned into haplotype-specific subsets (HP:i:1 / HP:i:2) to enable allele-specific methylation calling downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allele-specific-read-partitioning))
- **allele-specific-short-read-alignment**: Use when scRNA-Seq reads from an F1 hybrid cross (e.g. CAST×B6) must be assigned to maternal/paternal alleles for allele-specific expression counting, and multi-mapping reads should be retained rather than discarded. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-specific-short-read-alignment))
- **allele-stratification-by-detection-efficiency**: Use when evaluating how per-allele data quality (fraction of detected targeted segments in chromatin tracing/FISH data) affects chromatin loop caller sensitivity or false-positive discovery, or when deciding a detection-efficiency cutoff before loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/allele-stratification-by-detection-efficiency))
- **allele-umi-count-analysis**: Use when allele-level UMI counts from single-cell 3' RNA-seq (Cell Ranger output, SNVs called with Freebayes) must be converted into per-cell genotype calls or two-genotype mixture inference, e.g. donor/host chimerism in transplant or mixing datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allele-umi-count-analysis))
- **allelecall-scaling-analysis**: Use when you need to quantify the computational scaling of chewBBACA AlleleCall (runtime vs CPU count, per-sample seconds-to-minutes behaviour) for a fixed set of draft genome FASTA files on a laptop or HPC cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allelecall-scaling-analysis))
- **allelic-bursting-independence-testing**: Use when you have allele-specific single-cell count matrices and want to test per-gene whether maternal/paternal bursting events are independent (logOR ≈ 0), positively correlated, or anti-correlated, with FDR control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-bursting-independence-testing))
- **allelic-count-matrix-generation-from-bam**: Use when starting from cell-barcoded BAM/CRAM files from a scRNA-seq experiment (e.g., 10x Genomics) and you need allele-specific AD/DP matrices to feed haplotype-aware CNA callers such as XClone's BAF module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-count-matrix-generation-from-bam))
- **allelic-differential-splicing-statistical-modeling**: Use when merged per-allele inclusion and skipping counts (IJC/SJC for reference and alternative alleles across samples) are available and the goal is to call significant ASAS events at FDR ≤ 10% from replicate or population-scale RNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-differential-splicing-statistical-modeling))
- **allelic-dmr-detection**: Use when nanopore reads carry haplotype tags (maternal/paternal) plus per-read CpG methylation calls, and the goal is to identify allele-specific methylation or allelic DMRs between homologous chromosomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/allelic-dmr-detection))
- **allelic-dropout-modeling-single-cell-wgs**: Use when a single-cell WGS CNA analysis must pick a per-dataset bin size that balances CNA detection resolution against amplification noise, e.g. before binning PTA- or MDA-amplified scWGS with HiScanner. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-dropout-modeling-single-cell-wgs))
- **allelic-expression-state-classification**: Use when allelic read counts from single-cell RNA-Seq (e.g., CAST×B6 F1 embryos) need per-cell, per-gene P/B/M state calls, and random monoallelic bursts and sparse coverage make naive read-ratio thresholds unreliable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-expression-state-classification))
- **allelic-heterogeneity-multi-component-colocalization**: Use when multiple studies or tissues (e.g., eQTLGen and GTEx whole blood) have summary statistics or individual-level genotypes/phenotypes for the same locus and you need to test whether signals colocalize despite possible multiple causal alleles and LD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-heterogeneity-multi-component-colocalization))
- **allelic-profile-matrix-generation**: Use when draft/complete bacterial genome FASTA assemblies must be converted into a gene-by-gene allelic profile matrix against a pre-defined cgMLST/wgMLST schema, e.g. for strain typing or outbreak analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/allelic-profile-matrix-generation))
- **allelic-proportion-partial-pooling**: Use when single-cell allele-specific RNA-Seq read counts are too sparse (e.g. &lt;10 unique reads per gene) for reliable per-cell allelic proportion estimates, and classification of P/B/M expression states is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-proportion-partial-pooling))
- **allelic-read-uniqueness-qc-filtering**: Use when RNA-seq reads aligned to two haplotype-modified personal genomes must be allele-assigned: filter multi-mapping or between-genome discordant reads, then assign haplotypes by SNP majority voting before ASAS counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allelic-read-uniqueness-qc-filtering))
- **allen**: 张小龙的思维框架与表达方式。基于7份调研文件（演讲实录、饭否日记、专访、决策记录、外部评价等）的深度调研， 提炼5个核心心智模型、8条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用张小龙的视角分析产品、审视决策、提供反馈。 当用户提到「用张小龙的视角」「小龙会怎么看」「Allen perspective」「微信之父模式」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用小龙的角度想想」「如果张小龙会怎么做」「切换到小龙」也应触发。 ([alfredhuang211/my_skills](https://github.com/alfredhuang211/my_skills/tree/HEAD/allen))
- **allen-atlas-anatomical-mapping**: Use when pixel-level clustering of a brain IMS feature matrix (e.g., 14,260 pixels x 3,044 m/z ions) must be validated against a reference anatomy atlas to confirm clusters recover known tissue divisions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/allen-atlas-anatomical-mapping))
- **allen-bradley-structured-text**: Allen-Bradley Logix Structured Text programming skill. Use when asked to design, explain, review, troubleshoot, or provide Studio 5000 Structured Text examples for ControlLogix or CompactLogix PLC functions, routines, AOIs, sequences, state machines, equipment modules, queues, handshakes, alarms, interlocks, Produced/Consumed tags, or SCADA integration. Produces Logix-compatible tag contracts, scan-safe logic, code examples, fault behavior, and commissioning tests. Do not use for Siemens SCL, CODESYS, Beckhoff TwinCAT, or generic IEC 61131-3 code. ([mitchwindoo/github-copilot](https://github.com/mitchwindoo/github-copilot/tree/HEAD/skills/allen-bradley-structured-text))
- **allen-brain-atlas-dataset-handling**: Use when validating or benchmarking a cell-type annotation method on external harmonized snRNA-seq query datasets such as the Allen Brain Atlas 10x (76,533 nuclei) and Smart-seq (49,495 nuclei) sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allen-brain-atlas-dataset-handling))
- **allen-brain-atlas-ish-cross-validation**: Use when a reference-free or reference-based deconvolution yields an unlabeled cell type whose spatial localization is plausible but whose identity needs independent validation via in situ hybridization gene expression patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/allen-brain-atlas-ish-cross-validation))
- **allen-brain-regions**: Look up and standardize brain region names with the Allen Institute's ontology (the regions of the Allen Mouse Brain Common Coordinate Framework, CCFv3, plus the human and developing mouse atlases), and pick a BrainGlobe reference atlas. Use when matching region names across datasets or papers, finding a region's acronym, id or parent regions, or choosing an atlas for registration. ([stanczykwyatt/brainhound](https://github.com/stanczykwyatt/brainhound/tree/HEAD/skills/allen-brain-regions) · ★1)
- **allen-cacm1983**: Temporal interval algebra for reasoning about time relationships in planning and knowledge representation ([curiositech/windags-skills](https://github.com/curiositech/windags-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/allen-cacm1983) · ★18 · 2 copies)
- **allergen-detection**: Phát hiện 14 chất gây dị ứng theo luật EU 1169/2011 trong menu PDF và xuất PDF mới đã gắn nhãn allergen chuẩn. Trigger ngay khi user upload/mention một file menu PDF và muốn: gắn nhãn allergen, kiểm tra compliance EU, "check dị ứng", "allergen menu", "EU 1169", "label menu", "đánh dấu chất gây dị ứng", hoặc cần chuẩn bị menu cho thị trường châu Âu. Dùng khi user paste đường dẫn PDF và nói "check allergen", "label này", "scan menu" — kể cả khi không đề cập tên skill. ([aibot88/sec_skill_store](https://github.com/aibot88/sec_skill_store/tree/HEAD/skills/claudskills/allergen-detection) · ★6)
- **allergen-management**: Create allergen management programs for foodservice. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with allergen-management related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/allergen-management) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **allergen-management**: Create a allergen management with structured process, quality checks, and system integration ([DatBeat/GIFMakerz](https://github.com/DatBeat/GIFMakerz/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/food-beverage/allergen-management) · 4 copies)
- **allergen-regler**: Hvordan AllergiScans regelmotor faktisk læser en deklaration — maskering, ordgrænser, spor, fuzzy-matchning og de kendte huller. Slå op, før du ændrer noget i matcher.py, allergens.yaml eller OCR-efterbehandlingen. ([ninkaninus/food-allergy-app](https://github.com/ninkaninus/food-allergy-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/allergen-regler))
- **allergens**: questions about allergies, dairy, nuts, gluten or other dietary needs ([flow-engineer/flow](https://github.com/flow-engineer/flow/tree/HEAD/examples/cafe/agent/skills/allergens) · ★51)
- **allergist**: Scan a draft for every symptom in the author's allergy list (AI-tell constructions, lexicon, fake-specific numbers, em-dash density), quote each hit, and rewrite only the flagged passages until a full re-scan is clean. Flags, never scores. Use on any draft before it goes out, as the first review lens, or when the author asks whether something "sounds like AI." ([shortrib-labs/content-loop](https://github.com/shortrib-labs/content-loop/tree/HEAD/plugins/review/skills/allergist))
- **allergist-immunologist**: Use when a task needs the judgment of an allergist/immunologist — interpreting a skin-prick or specific-IgE result against pretest probability, working up a "penicillin allergy" label for delabeling before a needed antibiotic, managing anaphylaxis and biphasic-reaction risk, stepping asthma therapy up or down, or evaluating suspected primary immunodeficiency. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/allergist-immunologist) · ★27)
- **allergy**: Manage allergy records including drug, food, and environmental allergies with severity tracking and medical alert integration. ([johnalbertini14-glitch/openclaw-skills](https://github.com/johnalbertini14-glitch/openclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/huifer/wellally-health-skills/skills/allergy) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **Allergy**: Log allergens, track reactions, and chart symptom patterns over time. Use when identifying triggers, preparing reports, or setting reminders. ([MacD1998/SkillSecurer](https://github.com/MacD1998/SkillSecurer/tree/HEAD/dataset/SkillTrustBench/secureSkills/case_00956) · ★1 · 6 copies)
- **allergy-manager**: Manages allergy profiles including food, environmental, and drug allergies. Tracks reactions, identifies cross-reactivity risks, provides seasonal allergy forecasts, and warns about allergen exposure. Use when the user reports allergies, asks about cross-reactions, or needs allergen avoidance guidance. ([vitaclaw/vitaclaw](https://github.com/vitaclaw/vitaclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/allergy-manager) · ★23)
- **allergy-reaction**: Use when a caller reports a possible allergic reaction in themselves or a dependent — hives, facial swelling, breathing difficulty, lip or mouth tingling, GI symptoms after ingestion, or any reaction following exposure to a known allergen or a newly introduced food or medication. Produces a three-way next-action decision — Call (emergency or hotline), Go (open pharmacy), or Wait (home observation) — with the Yorukusu safety boundary on every output. Integrates the caller's Personal Health Context for known allergens, prior-reaction severity, and carried rescue medication. Never outputs a diag… ([Guttyo/yorukusu](https://github.com/Guttyo/yorukusu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/allergy-reaction) · ★1)
- **allergy-reaction-log**: Suit les réactions allergiques ou suspectées, identifie les déclencheurs et prépare un dossier structuré pour l'allergologue. À utiliser quand l'utilisateur décrit des réactions cutanées, respiratoires ou digestives suspectes. Se déclenche aussi avec "réaction allergique", "urticaire", "j'ai gonflé", "allergie", "éternuements", "yeux qui piquent", "eczéma", ou toute mention de réaction inexpliquée après exposition. Also triggers on "allergy log", "track allergic reactions", "find my allergy triggers". ([vantaflow/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/vantaflow/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/health-skills/allergy-reaction-log) · ★30 · 2 copies)
- **alley-oop**: Manages token-efficient session transitions in Claude Code. Use this skill whenever the user runs /pass, /catch, /passin, or /catchon, or says anything like "wrap up the session", "end the session", "save a handoff", "start fresh", "pick up where we left off", or "the session is getting long". The handoff document is a cold-start prompt written TO a fresh Claude instance — not a history dump. The goal is short sessions, low token cost, and clean continuity. ([1azarus/alley-oop](https://github.com/1azarus/alley-oop/tree/HEAD/skills/alley-oop))
